\chapter{Relationship register}\label{model:relationships}
An edge has its own assertion status. Canonical endpoints do not promote a Proposed edge. Unresolved associations are retained non-edges. Enablement does not mean decomposition, runtime dependency, implementation equivalence or transfer of originating authority; mappings are not inherited by descendants.

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Governed Information Operations; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance}) & \nameref{cap:ec-06} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-06}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Trust, Accountability \& Assurance; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance}) & \nameref{cap:ec-07} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-07}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Trust, Accountability \& Assurance; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction}) & \nameref{cap:ec-08} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-08}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Coordinated Activity \& Interaction; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction}) & \nameref{cap:ec-09} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-09}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Coordinated Activity \& Interaction; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction}) & \nameref{cap:ec-10} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-10}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Coordinated Activity \& Interaction; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-coordinated-activity-interaction}) & \nameref{cap:ec-11} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-11}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Coordinated Activity \& Interaction; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance}) & \nameref{cap:ec-12} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-12}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Trust, Accountability \& Assurance; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-governed-information-operations} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-governed-information-operations}) & \nameref{cap:ec-13} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-13}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Governed Information Operations; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance}) & \nameref{cap:ec-14} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-14}) & Proposed

The established reusable responsibility is a constituent ability of Trust, Accountability \& Assurance; its canonical scope and exclusions remain unchanged.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-provider-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-provider-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-practitioner} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-practitioner}) & Proposed

Practitioner identity and professional profile information let care participants be recognised across organisations. Registration and profile standing remain attributed to the issuing authority, while discovery makes that described person usable in care integration.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-provider-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-provider-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-practitioner-role} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-practitioner-role}) & Proposed

The same practitioner may act in different specialties, organisations and operational capacities. Role-specific associations and communication endpoints make that participation intelligible without converting a directory role into permission or professional privilege.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-provider-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-provider-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-provider-relationship} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-provider-relationship}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Effective affiliations explain which practitioner or team participates with which organisation. These relationships support collaboration and routing while each participating identity keeps its own responsibility owner.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-provider-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-provider-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-provider-engagement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-provider-engagement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Engagement information represents the terms and externally granted practice privileges under which a provider participates. This differs from who the practitioner is and from whether a particular operation is authorised now.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-organisation-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-organisation-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-healthcare-organisation} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-healthcare-organisation}) & Proposed

An identifiable legal or operational healthcare organisation needs verified identifiers, a profile and published standing. This description anchors service and affiliation relationships without managing the organisation itself.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-organisation-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-organisation-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-organisational-structure} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-organisational-structure}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Departments, divisions and regional networks need qualified structural relationships that can be traversed and understood over time. Structural membership is distinct from physical location and from authority to manage another organisation.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-organisation-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-organisation-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-organisation-contact} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-organisation-contact}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Verified facility contact channels make an organisation reachable for appropriate administrative and clinical communication. The contact record says how it may be reached; recipient qualification and disclosure policy govern a particular use.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-location-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-location-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-healthcare-location} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-healthcare-location}) & Proposed

Addresses, geospatial context and facility identity establish which physical place an information or activity concern refers to. Describing a location remains separate from deciding what services can operate there now.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-location-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-location-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-location-structure} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-location-structure}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Campus, building, floor, ward and room relationships explain physical containment. The structure provides navigation and context without determining organisational reporting lines or current operational capacity.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-location-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-location-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-place-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-place-definition}) & Proposed

A particular bed, bay, treatment chair or procedural place has stable physical characteristics relevant to care. Those descriptive capabilities remain separate from occupancy, cleaning state and suitability for a current patient.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-health-service-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-service-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-healthcare-service-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-healthcare-service-definition}) & Proposed

An offered service has an identifiable clinical or administrative purpose, specialty and published intake description. That definition is distinct from the provider/location binding through which the service can be delivered.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-health-service-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-service-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-service-provision-relationship} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-service-provision-relationship}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Service provision joins a named offering to the organisations, places and practitioner roles through which it is provided. The mapping is the owned concern; the identity and meaning of each mapped participant remain with its established capability.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-health-service-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-service-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-service-availability-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-service-availability-definition}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Regular hours, on-call windows and exceptional closures describe when an offering is nominally available. They provide an input to readiness and scheduling without proving that all required dependencies are ready.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-health-service-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-service-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-service-eligibility-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-service-eligibility-definition}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Published catchment, referral and clinical eligibility conditions explain the intended audience and access constraints of an offering. Their representation supports responsible assessment without deciding acceptance or clinical priority.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-health-product-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-product-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-health-product-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-product-definition}) & Proposed

Medication, blood-product, implant and consumable descriptions provide reference context for care information. Product identity and classification are retained without turning this reference responsibility into procurement or prescribing.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-health-product-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-product-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-product-formulary-catalogue} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-product-formulary-catalogue}) & Proposed

An approved facility formulary or product catalogue provides a qualified set of products relevant to local care. Representing its membership and source standing remains distinct from deciding formulary approval and from generic terminology governance.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-health-product-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-product-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-product-batch-serial-tracking} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-product-batch-serial-tracking}) & Proposed

Batch, lot and serial references make a product instance or supply-chain association traceable when care information needs it. This reference scope does not run inventory, recall operations or the supply chain.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-device-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-device-definition}) & Proposed

A device type has a model, regulatory description and stated capabilities used to interpret equipment context. This type-level responsibility differs from identifying the actual physical instance that produced information.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-device-instance} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-device-instance}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

An identifiable physical device carries UDI and serial context through its use. The instance record supports association and resolution without originating device observations or making maintenance decisions.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-device-association} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-device-association}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

A device can be associated with different patients and care places at different times. Qualified association and its lineage allow observations to be interpreted against the applicable binding rather than the current assignment alone.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-clinical-device-administration}) & \nameref{cap:bec-device-connectivity} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-device-connectivity}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

A device communication binding identifies the protocol and endpoint through which its feed can participate. Connectivity metadata supports routing without owning authentication issuance, payload semantics or physiological interpretation.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-access-transaction-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-access-transaction-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Coordinate the intake, care-request disposition and appointment information through which a person accesses care. Its constituent abilities distinguish Client-Centred Administration; Referral Administration; Scheduling Administration.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-fulfilment-administration-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-fulfilment-administration-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Maintain distinguishable care-episode, bounded fulfilment and delivered-activity information across performing systems. Its constituent abilities distinguish Episode \& Encounter Administration; Order Administration; Diagnostic Administration; Medication Administration; Procedure Administration.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-documentation-communication-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-documentation-communication-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Administer the lifecycle, shared-plan context and directed communication of care documentation. Its constituent abilities distinguish Clinical Record Administration; Care Plan Administration; Clinical Communication Administration.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-administration-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-financial-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-financial-information-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Represent coverage context and convey care-activity evidence to responsible funding and charging systems. Its constituent abilities distinguish Funding \& Coverage Administration; Clinical Charging \& Claims Administration.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-context-integration-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-context-integration-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-longitudinal-community-care-integration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-longitudinal-community-care-integration}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Support continuity, remote participation and preventive follow-up through appropriately assembled and exchanged care context. Its constituent abilities distinguish Primary Care; Community Care; Outreach Care; Remote \& Virtual Care; Preventive Care.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-context-integration-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-context-integration-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-facility-episodic-care-integration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-facility-episodic-care-integration}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Provide the context assemblies and boundary integration needed for facility presentations and bounded care episodes. Its constituent abilities distinguish Acute Care; Emergency Care; Inpatient Care; Ambulatory Care; Surgical \& Procedural Care.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-context-integration-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-context-integration-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-specialist-clinical-context-integration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-specialist-clinical-context-integration}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Integrate specialist assessment, investigation and therapeutic information with longitudinal and episode context. Its constituent abilities distinguish Clinical Assessment; Diagnostic Services; Therapeutic Services; Medication Therapy; Allied Health; Rehabilitation.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-setting-operations-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-setting-operations-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Coordinate the local flow, session state and capacity semantics of a care-delivery setting. Its constituent abilities distinguish Clinic \& Practice Operations; Ward Operations; Theatre Operations; Emergency Department Operations; Outpatient Operations.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-resource-capacity-coordination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-resource-capacity-coordination}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Relate operational care-place and equipment state to the coordinated view of service demand and capacity. Its constituent abilities distinguish Bed \& Care-Place Management; Clinical Resource Management; Service Capacity Management.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-operational-workforce-coordination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-operational-workforce-coordination}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Make workforce availability, coverage and eligible operational work participation usable in coordination. Its constituent abilities distinguish Clinical Workforce Management; Clinical Rostering; Mobile Staff Management; On-Call Management; Work Allocation \& Dispatch.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-workforce-qualification-development-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-workforce-qualification-development-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Represent learning, competence, professional-standing checks and separately governed operational access qualifications. Its constituent abilities distinguish Clinical Training \& Education; Clinical Competency Management; Clinical Qualification Management; Clinical Supervision; Credential Management.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-operations-coordination-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-care-movement-transition-coordination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-movement-transition-coordination}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Coordinate movement of people/materials and operational departure readiness across care boundaries. Its constituent abilities distinguish Patient Transport; Clinical Logistics Coordination; Discharge Management.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-clinical-information-stewardship-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-clinical-information-stewardship-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Maintain governed longitudinal synthesis and the associations needed to use its constituent clinical information safely. Its constituent abilities distinguish Patient Clinical Record.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-health-information-connectivity-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-health-information-connectivity-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Make governed health information discoverable and exchangeable under explicit internal and external contracts. Its constituent abilities distinguish Health Information Exchange; Health Information Access; Health Information Communication.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-semantic-reference-knowledge-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-semantic-reference-knowledge-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Govern the definitions, terminology and reference content that make healthcare information consistently interpretable. Its constituent abilities distinguish Clinical Knowledge Services; Information Design Governance.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Apply information-use policy and preserve the accountability context needed for governed health-information activity. Its constituent abilities distinguish Health Information Control.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governed-health-information-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Provide existing health information for authorised research, required population notification and service planning purposes. Its constituent abilities distinguish .\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-human-collaboration-work-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-human-collaboration-work-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-collaborative-interaction-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-collaborative-interaction-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Support contextual discussion and human interaction with governed information and activity. Its constituent abilities distinguish Clinical Collaboration; General Collaboration; Presentation Services.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-human-collaboration-work-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-human-collaboration-work-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-operational-activity-temporal-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-operational-activity-temporal-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Progress governed operational work in identifiable activity and temporal contexts. Its constituent abilities distinguish Workflow \& Activity Coordination; Calendar Management.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-independent-service-assurance-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-independent-service-assurance-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-assurance-specification-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-assurance-specification-enablement}) & Proposed

Model how satisfaction will be assured and maintain applicable, temporally qualified criteria. Its constituent abilities distinguish Assurance Design; Assurance Criteria Management.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-independent-service-assurance-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-independent-service-assurance-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-independent-assurance-evaluation-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-independent-assurance-evaluation-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Associate trustworthy evidence, assess it and adjudicate independently reportable findings about governed service behaviour. Its constituent abilities distinguish Governed Assurance.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-clinical-information-stewardship-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-clinical-information-stewardship-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-associated-clinical-binary-content-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-associated-clinical-binary-content-enablement}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Logical information may refer to one or several payloads held internally or by an external custodian. This capability keeps association and custody knowledge explicit so that content can be obtained under the governing information contract.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-clinical-information-stewardship-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-clinical-information-stewardship-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-information-authority-representation} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-information-authority-representation}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

A persisted assertion may be authoritative, informational or anecdotal. This ability carries that standing into interpretation without treating transport reliability, freshness or successful commit as proof of credibility.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-semantic-reference-knowledge-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-semantic-reference-knowledge-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-governed-reference-dataset-administration} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governed-reference-dataset-administration}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Value-set membership, mapping content and governed nomenclature may change independently of the structural definition that gives them meaning. Updates need identifiable review and effective standing without redefining model libraries as mutable data.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-significant-activity-audit-capture} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-significant-activity-audit-capture}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Mandatory evidence capture has its own policy-governed platform authority and must remain possible when the audited identity is denied an action. The captured fact is distinguished from debug output and the identity whose activity is recorded.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-information-lineage-stewardship} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-information-lineage-stewardship}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Recipients need to know which assertions were received, transformed or superseded and why. This ability preserves those meaningful relationships without making every retry or active-state reconstruction permanent provenance.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-authorised-research-information-provision} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-authorised-research-information-provision}) & Proposed

Research support must retain the purpose, applicable approvals and release qualification of supplied information. It reuses governed retrieval and privacy controls rather than claiming a clinical-trial or research-governance platform.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-population-health-notification-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-population-health-notification-enablement}) & Proposed

A reportable event and the obligation to notify are different from sending a message. This capability preserves the established obligation and observes the notification outcome under its contract without deciding public-health response.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-secondary-health-information-use-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-service-planning-information-provision} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-service-planning-information-provision}) & Proposed

Planners need comparable time- and service-qualified observations from multiple operating settings. This ability prepares those projections while leaving the interpretation of required future capacity and distribution with the planning authority.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-information-protection-accountability-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-governance-requirement-publication-support} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governance-requirement-publication-support}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Consumers need an identifiable statement of which rule applies and who established it. This proposal fills BC-17 information support without giving the platform authority to make legislation, approve governance requirements or reinterpret their force.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-operational-activity-temporal-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-operational-activity-temporal-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-entity-specific-operational-coordination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-entity-specific-operational-coordination}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Activity concerning the same patient, practitioner or bed needs a coherent entity-relative frame. Interest fan-out, serial progression, fresh information context and uncertain effects remain distinguishable from generic multi-work-unit progression.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-operational-activity-temporal-enablement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-operational-activity-temporal-enablement}) & \nameref{cap:bec-governed-operational-activity-design} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-governed-operational-activity-design}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

An activity needs a fulfilment definition even when only one bounded execution unit is involved. This capability specifies its semantic envelope while execution and event delivery remain separate responsibilities.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bec-care-resource-capacity-coordination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-care-resource-capacity-coordination}) & \nameref{cap:bec-service-offering-readiness-coordination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bec-service-offering-readiness-coordination}) & Historical assertion / Proposed

Collective readiness depends on suitable workforce, spaces, equipment, materials and supporting services. This proposal evaluates their joint availability for an offering without treating one available bed, an operating-hours declaration or aggregate capacity as service readiness.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bc-clinical-determination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-clinical-determination}) & \nameref{cap:bc-clinical-finding-interpretation} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-clinical-finding-interpretation}) & Proposed

Interpret observed clinical findings and their significance for the individual care concern. The proposed decomposition separates this outcome from the other responsibilities within Clinical Determination.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bc-clinical-determination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-clinical-determination}) & \nameref{cap:bc-diagnosis-problem-determination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-diagnosis-problem-determination}) & Proposed

Establish and qualify the clinical problems or diagnoses applicable to an individual. The proposed decomposition separates this outcome from the other responsibilities within Clinical Determination.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bc-clinical-determination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-clinical-determination}) & \nameref{cap:bc-health-risk-prognostic-determination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-health-risk-prognostic-determination}) & Proposed

Establish clinically meaningful risk and expected-course judgements for an individual. The proposed decomposition separates this outcome from the other responsibilities within Clinical Determination.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bc-individual-care-planning} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-individual-care-planning}) & \nameref{cap:bc-care-goal-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-care-goal-definition}) & Proposed

Establish the intended outcomes and priorities against which an individual care response is planned. The proposed decomposition separates this outcome from the other responsibilities within Individual Care Planning.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bc-individual-care-planning} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-individual-care-planning}) & \nameref{cap:bc-care-response-planning} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-care-response-planning}) & Proposed

Determine the intended clinical response and its sequencing in relation to agreed care goals. The proposed decomposition separates this outcome from the other responsibilities within Individual Care Planning.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bc-medication-pharmacy-services} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-medication-pharmacy-services}) & \nameref{cap:bc-medication-compounding-preparation} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-medication-compounding-preparation}) & Proposed

Prepare or compound a clinically required medication formulation under the applicable professional and product-governance mandate. The proposed decomposition separates this outcome from the other responsibilities within Medication \& Pharmacy Services.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:bc-care-access-coordination} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-care-access-coordination}) & \nameref{cap:bc-service-delivery-preparedness-management} (p.\,\pageref{cap:bc-service-delivery-preparedness-management}) & Proposed

Preparedness asks whether the service as a whole can operate, distinct from scheduling capacity or readiness of a single place. This is a proposed missing business frame for the already identified readiness concern.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-04} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-04}) & \nameref{cap:ec-active-information-availability} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-active-information-availability}) & Proposed

Active representation, observation and convergence support availability while preserving an explicit trust basis for use.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-04} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-04}) & \nameref{cap:ec-authoritative-durable-state-establishment} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-authoritative-durable-state-establishment}) & Proposed

Durable acceptance gives managed information an explicit committed state from which applicable active representations can be reconstructed.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-04} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-04}) & \nameref{cap:ec-binary-content-association-resolution} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-binary-content-association-resolution}) & Proposed

Document attachments, diagnostic media and operational artefacts share a need to locate usable content while distinguishing logical meaning from physical possession.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-06} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-06}) & \nameref{cap:ec-operation-policy-resolution} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-operation-policy-resolution}) & Proposed

Permission depends on controlled criteria, role assignments, action and information scope; revocation is distinct from natural expiry.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-06} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-06}) & \nameref{cap:ec-operation-bound-policy-enforcement} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-operation-bound-policy-enforcement}) & Proposed

Distributed participants apply the applicable effective authorisation and fail closed when authority is absent or untrustworthy.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-07} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-07}) & \nameref{cap:ec-information-attribution-lineage} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-information-attribution-lineage}) & Proposed

Lineage explains how an assertion reached its present form without turning every infrastructure event into provenance.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-07} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-07}) & \nameref{cap:ec-governed-activity-audit-evidence} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-governed-activity-audit-evidence}) & Proposed

Denied actions and successful mutations may both require trustworthy evidence; the audited identity and capture authority remain distinct.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-10} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-10}) & \nameref{cap:ec-operational-fulfilment-definition} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-operational-fulfilment-definition}) & Proposed

The semantic execution envelope applies to one-step and multi-step activity and stays distinct from dispatch and supervision.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-10} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-10}) & \nameref{cap:ec-governed-activity-execution-control} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-governed-activity-execution-control}) & Proposed

Reusable execution control respects work-unit archetype, participation and uncertain effects rather than equating dispatch with completion.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-10} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-10}) & \nameref{cap:ec-entity-relative-operational-progression} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-entity-relative-operational-progression}) & Proposed

Patient, practitioner and bed concerns recur across different business contexts; each can need a coherent progression frame without one central entity service.\\\hline
\nameref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-trust-accountability-assurance}) & \nameref{cap:ec-information-authority-qualification} (p.\,\pageref{cap:ec-information-authority-qualification}) & Proposed

Identity correlation, clinical-history synthesis, reference-content use and assurance evidence all need to distinguish the standing of an assertion from technical acceptance. This recurring ability is insufficiently explicit in the existing fourteen EC definitions and is proposed as a new peer responsibility.\\\hline
